- Bishop c4 attacks g8, f7, e6, d5... doesn't attack e8.
- Bishop b2 attacks h8, g7... doesn't attack e8.

- Bishop c4... doesn't attack c8.
- Bishop b2... doesn't attack c8.

So f8 is attacked? Let's see: h6 is (8, 6), f8 is (6, 8).
Delta x = -2, Delta y = +2. Yes, it's a diagonal.
So the King cannot move to f8. (Not a legal move anyway).

h6 is (8,6), e8 is (5,8).
Delta x = -3, Delta y = +2. Not a diagonal.
Is d8 attacked?
h6 is (8,6), d8 is (4,8).
Delta x = -4, Delta y = +2. Not a diagonal.
Is c8 attacked?
h6 is (8,6), c8 is (3,8).
Delta x = -5, Delta y = +2. Not a diagonal.
